The analyzer reads that cache read-only and derives, per 1:1 contact: who the contact is (customer, staff, family, friend, unknown), how the bot should address a customer, which catalog services they mentioned or likely used, and the last one they likely used.

- The analysis is rules only, on the Mac, with no network and no model call. Contacts never consented to sending their past chats to a model provider (Law 91/2025; `research/07-vn-legal-compliance.md`). `PROFILE_LLM_ENABLED` (env of the analyzer, default `false`) is reserved for a future model pass that would need consent; it is not implemented in this analyzer, and setting it to `true` makes it exit with code 2. Since 2026-11-12 the separate visits analyzer `scripts/analyze_visits.py` has an optional, consent-scoped model pass behind the same variable (section 15.6).
- Only derived facts reach the model, and only for a consented customer: `ClaimedJob.customer_profile` (section 3). Never history, snippets, stats, reasons, the display name, or a service of the catalog groups `health` / `women`.
- The bot must never tell the customer it read their history (instruction in the `<customer_profile>` block).
- Evidence snippets (at most 3 per contact, at most 80 characters, phone numbers and digit runs of 7+ digits masked, never from a message with health vocabulary or about a sensitive service) stay on the machine: the analyzer JSON file (mode 0600) and the local D1, shown to staff in the console.
- Message texts and names are never printed or logged by the analyzer; `audit_log` rows of profiles hold counts, field names and enum values only.

### Analyzer: `scripts/analyze_contacts.py`

`python3 -I scripts/analyze_contacts.py [--db PATH] [--owner-id ID] [--since YYYY-MM-DD] [--dry-run] [--push] [--server-url URL] [--out-dir DIR] [--catalog PATH]`

- Default db: the `active` account in `~/.zalo-agent-cli/accounts.json` (`ZALO_CONFIG_DIR` / `ZALO_DATA_DIR` respected), file `accounts/<ownId>/zalo.db`, opened with `file:...?mode=ro` and `PRAGMA query_only`.
- Threads: `threads.type = 'dm'` (or no thread row and a message sent by the thread id); groups, the thread equal to the own id, and thread ids outside `[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}` (the server would refuse the batch) are skipped and counted in `skipped`, and so are Zalo system and official accounts (`skipped.system_account`: a display name containing "Zalo", "Official Account" or the word "OA", or a contact whose rows are only cards, events and links with at least one card or event). Contact rows: `senderId = threadId`; every other sender is the owner. System rows (`deleted`, `event`, `group_event`, `poll_event`, cards, `ge:` ids) are not counted; text analysis uses `type = 'text'` rows.
- Output: `$THOMHOUSE_BOT_HOME/profiles/contacts-<YYYYMMDD-HHMM>.json` (default `~/.thomhouse-bot/profiles`, dir 0700, file 0600): `{ analyzer_version, generated_at, since, owner_id, contacts, skipped, profiles: [CustomerProfileImport] }`. A folder given with `--out-dir` is set to 0700 only when the analyzer creates it; an existing one keeps its mode, with a warning on stderr when group or other users can read it. Stdout: counts only. `--dry-run` writes and pushes nothing. `analyzer_version` is `contacts-rules-v3` since profiles v2 (2026-11-08; `contacts-rules-v2` after the first review fixes the same day).
- `--push`: `POST <server>/staff/profiles/import` in batches of 200 with `Authorization: Bearer <STAFF_TOKEN>` and `X-Staff-Id: analyzer`. Server: `--server-url`, else `SERVER_URL` (env, then `apps/server/.dev.vars`), else `http://127.0.0.1:8787`; a host other than `localhost`, `127.0.0.1`, `::1`, `*.localhost`, `*.test` is refused. `STAFF_TOKEN` from the env or `apps/server/.dev.vars` (`THOMHOUSE_DEV_VARS` overrides the path; never printed).
- Heuristics (pronoun roles, recommendation, relationship scores, service aliases, past-use signals) are documented in the module docstring. Summary: the bot always calls itself `em`; `address_term` is the senior term (`chị`, `anh`, `cô`, `chú`, `bác`) the owner uses for the contact or the contact uses for themselves; when the owner calls the contact `em`/`con`/`cháu`, the contact's own self-term is used only if it is `anh`/`chị` from a clear pattern ("cho chị hỏi", "anh muốn ..."), else `anh/chị`; no evidence gives `anh/chị` with `low` confidence. Gender-neutral pairs ("anh/chị", "anh chị", "a/c") count for neither person. In the contact's clauses with two pronouns, "PRON1 ... giúp/cho PRON2" ("anh xem giúp em") and a senior PRON1 before a junior PRON2 ("chị gửi em địa chỉ") read PRON1 as the owner; a wrong reading gives the contact a junior self-term and so the neutral `anh/chị`, never a gendered term. The owner's own clauses of that shape with a senior PRON1 are not counted, since either reading could hand the contact the owner's own term. An elder (the owner calls themselves `con`/`cháu`, or the contact calls the owner `con`/`cháu`) without a direct `cô`/`chú`/`bác` gets `bác` when the owner calls them `ông`/`bà`, `cô` for a middle name "Thị", `chú` for "Văn", else `anh/chị`.
- Relationship (analyzer v3; kinship and insider evidence outweigh service words, since people around a spa owner talk about the spa all the time):
  - Kinship terms are pronouns: family-only terms (`mẹ`, `má`, `ba`, `bố`, `bô`, `cha`, plain `me`, `nội`, `ngoại`, `dì`, `dượng`, `mợ`, `thím`, `vợ`, `chồng`, `vk`, `ck` only as a vocative), elder-or-peer terms (`ông`, `bà`), child terms (`con`, `cháu`), endearments (`bé`, `cưng`). A kin term followed by a pronoun or relative word is a third person ("mẹ em", "con chị", "vợ anh", "ba mẹ", "bà bầu", "ông xã"). Every term in a pronoun position is counted per side (`addressing.uses_owner`, `addressing.uses_contact`), whoever it points at.
  - `family`: uses of family-only terms (3+), plus child terms up to that number, couple signals ("anh yêu", "vợ iu", "ông xã ơi"), family endearments ("con yêu") and a thread name that is only a family word ("Mẹ", "Vợ iu"), reach 6+ points and 1.5 x the uses of non-kin terms (`chị`, `anh`, `em`, `mình`, `bạn`, `tôi`, `tớ`, `tui`, `tao`, `mày`); `high` at 12+ points, 3 x the non-kin uses and family terms from both sides. An elder pattern without family-only terms, with personal chat and no buying, is `family`/`medium`. Reason `family_role:contact_parent|contact_child|spouse|elder|relative`.
  - `staff`: the contact talks about the shop as an insider (ca làm, nghỉ ca, chấm công, ứng lương, dọn tiệm, KTV, chia tour, xin nghỉ; the shop's customers as a third person: "khách đặt/tới/hủy/hỏi", "có khách", "lịch của khách", "báo/nhắn/chuyển khoản cho khách") in 2+ messages, with 6+ staff points (2 per contact insider message, 0.5 per weak one such as "đi làm", "tới tiệm", 1 per owner instruction, 0.5 per owner insider message, 2 when the owner calls the contact `bé` twice), at least half the customer points, and the contact is the junior (called `em`/`bé`, calls the owner `chị`/`anh`) or talks shop in 5+ messages; `high` at 5+ insider messages, 15+ points, junior evidence 6+ and 2 x the senior evidence, and staff points at least the customer points. A message about the shop's operations is work talk: its services, prices and bookings are not the contact's.
  - `friend`: a very long chat (1000+ text messages, or 400+ over 30+ days) or a long one (200+ over 10+ days) with intimacy or chit-chat, with a purchase ratio (contact messages with customer words / contact text messages) under 3 %, no shop/ad address and fewer than 2 insider messages, or 2+ `tao`/`mày` with 30+ messages and no customer word from the contact, is `high`; a long chat under 8 %, or personal points 2 x the customer points, is `medium`.
  - `customer`: customer points as before (strong words, catalog services, shop/ad address, the owner's "dạ ... chị/anh"), without kinship, insider or friend dominance. Order: family, staff, elder family, friend high, friend medium, customer, staff low, friend low, family low, unknown.
  - Family, staff and friends get `address_term: null` with `address_confidence: 'low'` (`addressing.bot_term_if_customer` keeps the recommendation), no evidence and no `last_service`.
- Names: emoji, digits, punctuation and decoration words (girl, boy, phố, xinh, cute, mr, mrs, ms, official, shop, spa, store, nail, khách quen, a district ...) are removed first; a business word gives no name; the last word is used only after a kinship prefix ("Chị", "C.", "A.", "Em", "Cô", "Chú"), as one common given name alone ("Vy girl phố" gives `Vy`), or in a 2-5 word family-first name.
- Services: only catalog services and catalog categories count; a message that names only a service the studio does not offer (làm móng, nail, nối mi, cắt tóc, triệt lông, xông hơi, ngâm chân ...) gives no service, no customer vocabulary and no evidence (reason `non_catalog_service:<n>`).
- `last_service`: the likely-used service with the latest past-use signal, `{ key, service_id, name, at, source: 'chat', confidence, sensitive }`; `high` after 2+ signals in messages naming the service, `medium` when the latest signal named it, `low` when it comes only from the 72-hour window around a visit phrase.

### Profile shapes (`src/types.ts`)

`CustomerProfileImport` (one analyzer profile):

```json
{ "zalo_user_id": "p:2001", "display_name": "Nguyễn Thị Lan",
  "relationship": "customer", "relationship_confidence": "medium", "relationship_reasons": ["service_vocab:3", "catalog_match:2"],
  "address_term": "chị", "name_to_use": "Lan", "address_confidence": "medium",
  "addressing": { "owner_calls_contact": { "chị": 4 }, "contact_calls_self": { "chị": 2 }, "contact_calls_owner": { "em": 3 },
                  "owner_calls_self": { "em": 2 }, "name_prefix": null, "basis": { "senior_direct": { "chị": 6 }, "junior_direct": 0 } },
  "services": [ { "key": "goi-dau-thu-gian", "service_id": "goi-dau-thu-gian", "name": "Gội đầu thư giãn", "group": "hair",
                  "sensitive": false, "status": "likely_used", "mention_count": 3, "last_mentioned_at": 1762000000000,
                  "used_signal_count": 1, "last_used_signal_at": 1762000000000 },
                { "key": "cat:massage", "service_id": null, "name": "massage", "group": "relaxation", "sensitive": false,
                  "status": "mentioned", "mention_count": 2, "last_mentioned_at": 1762000000000, "used_signal_count": 0, "last_used_signal_at": null } ],
  "evidence": [ { "at": 1762000000000, "from": "contact", "kind": "service", "text": "Chị muốn đặt lịch gội đầu thư giãn thứ 7" } ],
  "last_service": { "key": "goi-dau-thu-gian", "service_id": "goi-dau-thu-gian", "name": "Gội đầu thư giãn", "at": 1762000000000,
                    "source": "chat", "confidence": "medium", "sensitive": false },
  "stats": { "messages_in": 10, "messages_out": 8, "text_in": 9, "text_out": 8, "first_at": 1760000000000, "last_at": 1762000000000,
             "first_writer": "contact", "active_days": 4, "service_messages": 6, "booking_messages": 2, "price_messages": 1,
             "visit_signal_messages": 1, "personal_signal_messages": 0, "customer_score": 9.5, "personal_score": 0 } }
```

Validation (422 `validation_failed`, `details.field` like `profiles[3].name_to_use`): `zalo_user_id` matches `^p:[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$`; `display_name` at most 100 characters or null; `relationship` `customer|staff|family|friend|unknown`, or the legacy `personal` (analyzer v2 files and older staff edits; analyzer v3 never writes it); both confidences `low|medium|high`; `relationship_reasons` at most 20 codes `[a-z0-9_:.-]{1,64}`; `address_term` `chị|anh|cô|chú|bác|anh/chị|null`; `name_to_use` one word of letters, at most 20 characters, or null; `addressing` and `stats` objects of at most 4000 JSON characters; `services` at most 40 entries (`key` = service id or `cat:<name>`, `service_id` `[a-z0-9-]{1,64}` or null, `name` 1-120 characters, `group` at most 32 or null, `sensitive` boolean, `status` `mentioned|likely_used`, counts non-negative integers, times ms or null; a `health`/`women` group is stored as sensitive whatever the flag says); `evidence` at most 3 entries (`at` ms, `from` `contact|owner`, `kind` `service|booking|price`, `text` 1-80 characters); `last_service` absent, null, or an object (`key` service id or `cat:<name>`, `service_id` `[a-z0-9-]{1,64}` or null, one of the two required, `name` 1-120 characters, `at` ms, `source` `chat` only, `confidence` `low|medium|high`, `sensitive` boolean or absent; `cat:dien_chan` and `cat:yoni` are stored as sensitive whatever the flag says).

Reason codes: `service_vocab:<n>` (contact messages with strong customer words), `catalog_match:<n>` (the contact naming catalog services), `booking_vocab:<n>`, `price_vocab:<n>`, `weak_service_vocab:<n>` (contact messages with everyday customer words only), `calls_owner_business:<n>` (the contact writes shop/ad/admin/spa), `owner_business:<n>` (the owner writes "shop"), `visit_signal:<n>`, `owner_service_vocab:<n>`, `owner_deference:<n>` (owner messages opening with "dạ/vâng" that call the contact chị/anh), `intimate_pronoun:<n>` (tao/mày, tui with ông/bà, tớ with cậu), `personal_chitchat:<n>`, `long_chat_no_service`, `many_days_no_service`; since analyzer v3 also `family_role:<role>`, `kin_display_name`, `kin_address:<n>` (uses of family-only terms), `kin_child_terms:<n>`, `couple_signal:<n>`, `kin_endearment:<n>`, `elder_kin_pattern`, `contact_elder:<n>` (the owner calls themselves con/cháu or is called so), `insider_contact:<n>`, `insider_contact_weak:<n>`, `insider_owner:<n>`, `owner_instruction:<n>`, `owner_calls_be:<n>`, `junior_pattern:<n>`, `senior_pattern:<n>`, `non_catalog_service:<n>`, `long_personal_chat`, `very_long_chat`, `purchase_ratio:<0.00>` (with 30+ contact text messages). `kinship_vocative:<n>` appears only in analyzer v2 profiles.

Category keys: `cat:goi_dau`, `cat:massage`, `cat:cham_soc_da`, `cat:da_mun`, `cat:quang_mat` (may reach the model with fixed labels), `cat:dien_chan`, `cat:yoni` (sensitive; staff only). Analyzer v3 emits a category only when the catalog has services of its group. `cat:triet_long`, `cat:xong_hoi`, `cat:ngam_chan`, `cat:nail` (services the studio does not offer) appear only in analyzer v2 profiles and never reach the model.

`CustomerProfileView` (what staff routes return):

```json
{ "zalo_user_id": "p:2001", "thread_id": "2001", "customer_id": "cus_..." , "conversation_id": "cnv_...",
  "display_name": "Nguyễn Thị Lan", "relationship": "customer", "relationship_confidence": "medium", "relationship_reasons": ["..."],
  "address_term": "chị", "name_to_use": "Lan", "address_confidence": "medium", "addressing": { "...": {} },
  "services": [ "ProfileService ..." ], "evidence": [ "ProfileEvidence ..." ], "stats": { },
  "analyzer": { "relationship": "customer", "relationship_confidence": "medium", "address_term": "chị", "name_to_use": "Lan",
                "address_confidence": "medium", "services": [ ] },
  "analyzer_version": "contacts-rules-v2", "source": "analyzer", "staff_override": false, "staff_fields": [], "staff_note": null,
  "analyzed_at": 1762600000000, "created_at": 1762600000000, "updated_at": 1762600000000, "updated_by": "analyzer",
  "last_service": { "key": "massage-chan", "service_id": "massage-chan", "name": "Massage chân", "at": 1762500000000,
                    "source": "visit", "confidence": "high", "sensitive": false },
  "model_facts": { "relationship": "customer", "address_term": "chị", "name_to_use": "Lan",
                   "services_likely_used": ["Gội đầu thư giãn"], "services_mentioned": ["massage"],
                   "last_service": { "name": "Massage chân", "date": "2026-11-07" } } }
```

`customer_id` / `conversation_id` are null until the contact writes to the account while the bot runs. `analyzer` is null for a profile staff created. `staff_fields` lists the field groups staff edited, a subset of `relationship` (with `relationship_confidence`), `address` (`address_term` with `address_confidence`), `name` (`name_to_use`) and `services`; `staff_override` is true exactly when it is not empty (column `staff_fields_json` of migration 0007). `last_service` merges the analyzer's chat signal (column `last_service_json` of migration 0008) with the newest staff-recorded visit of the customer (`visits` joined through `customers.zalo_user_id`, written by `POST /staff/visits`): the newer one wins, a visit wins a tie; a visit gives `source: 'visit'`, `confidence: 'high'`, the active catalog's name, and `sensitive` true for a `health`/`women` service or one the catalog does not know. A chat signal whose service is no longer in `services` with status `likely_used` (staff edited the list) is dropped. Null when there is neither. `model_facts` is exactly what a draft job for this customer would carry now (null when nothing would be sent).

### Staff routes (staff auth, `X-Staff-Id` recorded as `updated_by`)

| Method | Path | Request | Response 200 | Errors |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/staff/profiles/import` | `{ "analyzer_version"?: "contacts-rules-v2", "generated_at"?: ms, "profiles": CustomerProfileImport[] }` (at most 500 profiles, body at most 2,000,000 bytes, counted while reading, so a chunked body without Content-Length is capped too) | `{ "imported", "created", "updated", "kept_staff_fields" }` | 400 not JSON, 413 too large, 422 (whole batch refused, nothing stored; duplicate ids too) |
| GET | `/staff/profiles` | query `relationship`, `confidence` (relationship confidence), `staff_override` (`true`/`false`), `limit` (default 50, max 200), `cursor` | `ListResponse<CustomerProfileView>`, last change first | 422 bad filter |
| GET | `/staff/profiles/:zalo_user_id` | path `p:<threadId>` (encode the colon as `%3A` or not) | `{ "profile": CustomerProfileView }` | 400 bad id, 404 |
| PATCH | `/staff/profiles/:zalo_user_id` | `ProfilePatchRequest`, any subset of `relationship`, `relationship_confidence`, `address_term` (or null), `name_to_use` (or null), `address_confidence`, `services` (full list, same shape as import), `staff_note` (at most 500, or null), `staff_override` | `{ "profile": CustomerProfileView }` | 400 bad id, 422 unknown field / bad value / empty body / `staff_override: false` together with field edits |
| GET | `/staff/conversations/:id` | — | `ConversationDetail` gains `profile: CustomerProfileView \| null` (looked up by `customer.zalo_user_id`) | — |

Before migration `0007` is applied (`wrangler dev` reloads code on its own), the customer path treats the missing `customer_profiles` table as "no profile" (claims carry `customer_profile: null`, the detail has `profile: null`, the quiet rule never fires, `GET /staff/profiles/:id` answers 404), and `POST /staff/profiles/import` with profiles, `GET /staff/profiles` and `PATCH` answer 409 `invalid_state` ("apply migration 0007_customer_profiles.sql").

Before migration `0008` is applied (0007 present): reads, claims and the quiet rule work (no `last_service_json` column means no chat `last_service`; visits still merge); `POST /staff/profiles/import` with profiles answers 409 `invalid_state` ("apply migration 0008_profiles_v2.sql"), because it writes `last_service_json`; a `PATCH` that sets `staff`, `family` or `friend` answers the same 409 (the 0007 CHECK constraint); other PATCHes work. Migration 0008 rebuilds `customer_profiles` (SQLite cannot change a CHECK constraint in place) and copies every row unchanged.

Import upsert: a new row gets `source = 'analyzer'`, `staff_override = 0`, `staff_fields = []`. An existing row always gets the new `display_name` (kept when the import has none), `relationship_reasons`, `addressing`, `evidence`, `stats`, `analyzer`, `analyzer_version`, `analyzed_at` (`generated_at`, else now). The six editable fields are taken per group: a group listed in `staff_fields` stays as staff left it, the other groups take the analyzer's values (so a contact whose name staff fixed still gets the analyzer's new relationship). `kept_staff_fields` counts rows with at least one staff group. One `audit_log` row `profile.import` with the counts.

PATCH rules: editing a field adds its group to `staff_fields` (`staff_override = 1`). `relationship` without `relationship_confidence` sets the confidence to `high` (staff confirmed); `address_term` without `address_confidence` sets `high` (or `low` when the term is cleared to null). Editing `name_to_use` alone changes neither confidence. `staff_override: true` alone pins all four groups at their current values. `staff_override: false` (alone, or with `staff_note`) clears `staff_fields` and restores the six fields from `analyzer` when the profile has one. `staff_note` alone does not change the groups. A PATCH on a missing profile creates it (`source = 'staff'`). Audit `profile.staff_edit` / `profile.override_cleared` with field names, group names and enum values only (never the name or the note).

### What the model gets (`customer_profile` in the claim, section 3)

Built by `profileFactsForModel` from the stored profile and the job's `catalog_version` (additive 2026-11-12: a `contact_roles` row is applied first; `manager` or `staff` gives `null`, `customer` reads the profile as relationship `customer`, section 14.3):

1. `relationship` other than `customer` or `unknown` (`staff`, `family`, `friend`, the legacy `personal`; any confidence): `null`, nothing at all.
2. `address_term`: only when `address_confidence` is `medium`/`high` (a term staff choose is stored as `high`; a staff edit of another field does not raise a `low` guess); else `null` (the default "anh/chị" of block A applies).
3. `name_to_use`: only together with a specific `address_term` (not `anh/chị`) and when it is one word of letters.
4. Services: names come from the active catalog's `services.display_name_vi` for entries with a `service_id`, or from the fixed labels of `cat:goi_dau|massage|cham_soc_da|da_mun|quang_mat`; entries marked sensitive, of the groups `health`/`women`, unknown to the catalog, or other categories are dropped; `likely_used` first, then `mentioned` without repeats, at most 5 each.
5. `last_service` (additive, profiles v2): `{ "name", "date": "YYYY-MM-DD" }` (local date, Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh), nothing else. Candidates: the newest recorded visit, and the chat signal when its confidence is `medium`/`high` (and its service is still listed as `likely_used`); the newer wins (a visit wins a tie). Nothing when the winner is sensitive (`health`/`women` group, `cat:dien_chan`/`cat:yoni`, or a service unknown to the catalog), or older than 180 days (`LAST_SERVICE_MODEL_MAX_AGE_MS`, 2026-11-08, for the `chao_khach_quen` reply template); the name follows rule 4. Absent when there is none.
6. Nothing left (no term, no services, no last service): `null`.

The runner renders it after `turn_context_text` as a `<customer_profile>` block (`profile_block()`): the address line ("call the customer "chị" instead of "anh/chị"; you are still "em""), the name line, "services this customer has probably used before (not certain)", "services this customer asked about before (not necessarily used)", the last service line ("- last service: Massage chân (07/11). Nhắc nhẹ dịch vụ lần trước khi chào hoặc khi phù hợp, không khẳng định nếu khách không nhắc lại."; `dd/mm`, with `/yyyy` when the year is not the current one), and the rules: never say or hint that it read or analysed earlier messages, a history or a profile; never quote the block; do not claim a service was used unless listed as probably used, and even then ask rather than state; when unsure, keep "anh/chị" and ask politely. Since 2026-11-08 (reply templates) the block also has a first-sentence line (address the customer as "<term> <name>" in the first sentence of every reply; `{xung_ho}` and `{ten}` values for the templates), a greeting template line (`chao_khach_quen` for `relationship = customer` or when a last service is present, else `chao_khach_moi`), the last service line followed by the filled `chao_khach_quen` sentence ("Lần trước chị làm … bên em hôm dd/mm, chị thấy sau buổi đó thế nào ạ?"), and an exception in the rules: the last service may be mentioned once, in a greeting, worded as that template. The runner re-checks the values (known terms only, name regex, at most 5 names, a last service only with a name of 1-120 characters and a valid `YYYY-MM-DD` date, tags neutralized) and renders nothing unless `relationship` is `customer` or `unknown`.

### The quiet rule for family, staff and friends (Conversation DO, personal channel only)

Additive 2026-11-12: the rule does not fire for a contact with a `contact_roles` row `customer`, and a `manager` or `staff` row stops ingest before this rule (section 14.3). On an incoming customer message while `bot_state = 'awaiting_consent'`, after the typed-consent check (a typed "đồng ý" to a delivered prompt is still honoured) and before the "gặp nhân viên" / prompt handling: when flag `profile_personal_skip_bot` is `true`, the profile of `customer.zalo_user_id` has `relationship` `family`, `staff`, `friend` or the legacy `personal`, with `relationship_confidence = 'high'` or, at any confidence, `staff_override = 1` (staff owns some field of the profile and left that relationship in place; `src/lib/profiles.ts` `quietProfile`), the customer has no granted `ai_assistant` consent, and staff has never released this conversation out of `staff_only` (an `audit_log` `conversation.release` row whose `before_json` holds `staff_only`), the DO sets `bot_state = 'staff_only'`, `needs_attention = 1`, clears takeover and debounce, cancels queued draft jobs, cancels queued bot and server rows of the conversation (a waiting consent prompt becomes `failed`, error `cancelled: personal contact (profile)`), writes audit `conversation.staff_only_profile` (actor `system`/`profile`), sends nothing, and returns `IngestResult.action = 'staff_only_profile'` (additive). Later messages hit the existing `staff_only` branch (stored, `needs_attention = 1`); while the same conditions hold, a "gặp nhân viên" from that contact is stored the same way too, with no fixed reply and no handoff (other `staff_only` conversations still get `STAFF_WILL_REPLY_TEXT` and a handoff). A consented customer (`bot_active`) is never affected. Staff undoes it with `POST /staff/conversations/:id/release` (back to `awaiting_consent`; the rule does not fire again for that conversation) or by correcting the profile (`PATCH ... { "relationship": "customer" }`) before the contact writes.

### Known gaps (contact profiles)

- Vietnamese pronoun roles are guessed from word order; questions whose subject could be either person are skipped, so a short chat often ends with `anh/chị` and `low` confidence. Staff corrects in the console.
- A shared account (two people on one Zalo) or a contact who writes for someone else mixes evidence; the confidence drops but the analyzer cannot tell.
- `likely_used` is a signal, not a visit record; the bot is told not to state it as fact.
- The analyzer runs only when the owner starts it; new contacts after the last run have no profile until the next run.
- Profiles v2 thresholds were tuned on synthetic chats that reproduce the owner's counts (a mother via "mẹ"/"con", a young staff member via "chị"/"em"/"bé" and work talk, a close friend with a long chat about the spa), not on the real `zalo.db`. Siblings and cousins who use `anh`/`chị`/`em` look like friends (no family-only term); an aunt called `cô` looks like an elder customer unless the chat is personal with no buying; staff older than the owner need 5+ insider messages; a supplier talking about orders is not recognised. Staff correct these in the console; a staff relationship is kept across imports.
- A customer who books for a relative without a possessive ("đặt cho mẹ") adds a kin use; it takes 3+ family-only uses that also dominate the chat's other address terms to make a family profile.
- Without a profile row, a customer's recorded visits do not reach the model (no `customer_profile` at all). Additive 2026-11-12: in a two-bot room the read tool `get_customer_profile` gives the recorded visits with or without a profile (section 14.6); the direct engine is unchanged.
- Console (added 2026-11-08; profiles v2 since the evening of 2026-11-08: relationship labels khách, nhân viên, gia đình, bạn bè, chưa rõ, plus "người quen (nhãn cũ)" for legacy rows, selectable only while a row still has it; a "Dịch vụ lần trước" line with date and source, chat signal with its confidence or a staff-recorded visit; the model facts line includes the last service; a 409 naming `0008_profiles_v2.sql` gets its own message; Settings lists `profile_personal_skip_bot`): `apps/console/src/api.ts` mirrors these types; `apps/console/src/screens/ProfileCard.tsx` shows the profile in the conversation detail of a personal conversation (`p:` id, or any detail with a `profile`). The form opens with the profile's values, except that a `low`-confidence address guess is left empty (with a hint), and saving PATCHes only the fields that changed, so only their groups become staff-owned. A separate button "Xác nhận quan hệ và cách gọi đang hiện" (shown while either confidence is below `high`) PATCHes the shown `relationship`, `address_term` and `name_to_use` to confirm them. The card lists `staff_fields`; the clear button sends `{ "staff_override": false }`. The console does not edit `services` or `staff_note`. The desktop CORS shim (`apps/desktop/main.js`) now allows `PATCH`.


## 14. Two-bot rooms, auto reply switch, contact roles and customer summary

Status: written 2026-11-12 before the code, and rewritten the same day after the build and the integration so that it describes what the code does; the decisions taken during the build are listed at the end of the section. Updated the evening of 2026-11-12 after the live run-through on the build Mac: the lead's rules file in the platform workspace and the first-reply line (14.8), late follow-up jobs (14.5), and the known gaps. Section 15 adds visits, benefits and phone numbers guessed from old chats to the summary of 14.6. Additive to sections 1, 3, 4 and 13. Code: `apps/server/migrations/0010_rooms_roles_customer.sql`, `apps/server/src/lib/{roles,customer-status,customer-summary,rooms,flags}.ts`, `apps/server/src/staff-api/customers.ts`, `apps/server/src/runner-api/rooms.ts`, the internal-contact checks in `src/conversation-do.ts`, `src/jobs/draft-jobs.ts`, `src/jobs/followup-jobs.ts`, `src/tools/index.ts`, `src/bridge/outbox.ts` and `src/scheduler/followups.ts`, `apps/runner/runner/{room_engine,room_tools,room_setup}.py`, `apps/zalo-bridge/bridge/{labels,rooms}.py`, `apps/console/src/screens/{ConversationSettings,CustomerSummaryCard,VisitFields}.tsx`, `scripts/start_local.sh`, `scripts/stop_local.sh`, `scripts/import_visits.py`. Tests: server `test/{migration-0010,roles,customer-status,customer-summary,visits-import,benefits-visits,auto-reply,internal-contact,rooms-feed,customer-profile-tool,pipeline-missing-0010}.test.ts`; runner `tests/test_rooms.py`, `tests/test_room_tools.py`; bridge `tests/test_labels.py`, `tests/test_rooms.py`, `tests/test_local_scripts.py`; scripts `scripts/tests/test_import_visits.py`; console `src/ConversationSettings.test.tsx`, `src/CustomerSummary.test.tsx`. Guide for the owner and the receptionist (Vietnamese): `docs/rooms.md`.

### 14.1 What it is and what never changes

Each one-to-one personal Zalo conversation of a consenting customer can get one room on a local OpenMausBot server (npm `openmausbot` 0.1.154, `tools/openmausbot`, 127.0.0.1:8799, data in `<THOMHOUSE_BOT_HOME>/openmausbot`, by default `~/.thomhouse-bot/openmausbot`). Two bots are members of every room: the business bot (`Bot dịch vụ`, the lead: services, prices, policies, reply style) and the customer bot (`Bot khách hàng`: everything recorded about this customer). For a draft turn the runner posts the customer's messages into the room, the lead asks the customer bot, reads the business tools and writes one reply; the runner checks that reply and submits it as an ordinary draft. Rooms run only when the server flag `rooms_enabled` is `true` **and** the runner has `THOMHOUSE_DRAFT_ENGINE=rooms`; either one alone changes nothing.

Invariants:

- The server flag `ai_engine` stays `subscription`. Which engine writes a draft is a runner setting (14.8); the server cannot tell the engines apart, except that a room draft carries `session_id = "room.<cnv_id>"`.
- A reply reaches Zalo only through `POST /runner/jobs/:id/draft` (checker, outbox, rate limits). The room platform never talks to Zalo and is never exposed beyond this machine.
- Nothing is sent without staff approval unless that conversation's auto reply switch is on (14.4). The switch is off (NULL) for every conversation until staff turn it on.
- Bots see only conversations whose customer granted `ai_assistant` consent, is not deleted, is not flagged as a minor and is not an internal contact (14.3, 14.7). Phone numbers and sensitive service names (section 13 rule 4) never reach a model. Free text typed by staff (technician, a benefit title that names no sensitive service) does reach the model (Known gaps).
- New response fields are optional and are emitted only when they have a value, so the exact-equality tests of sections 3 and 13 keep passing. With flag `rooms_enabled = false` (default), no `auto_reply` value and no `contact_roles` row, every existing behaviour is unchanged.

### 14.2 Migration `0010_rooms_roles_customer.sql`

```sql
-- visits: rebuild to add amount, technician, duration, the service name as recorded, and the 'import' source
CREATE TABLE visits_v10 (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  customer_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES customers(id),
  service_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  service_name TEXT,
  visited_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  duration_min INTEGER CHECK (duration_min IS NULL OR duration_min > 0),
  amount_vnd INTEGER CHECK (amount_vnd IS NULL OR amount_vnd >= 0),
  technician TEXT,
  benefit_id TEXT,
  source TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'staff' CHECK (source IN ('staff','booking_app','booking_request','import')),
  external_visit_id TEXT UNIQUE,
  booking_request_id TEXT,
  recorded_by TEXT,
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
);
-- copy the columns that exist in the old table, DROP TABLE visits, RENAME, then:
CREATE INDEX idx_visits_customer ON visits(customer_id, visited_at DESC);

CREATE TABLE customer_benefits (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  customer_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES customers(id),
  kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('package','voucher')),
  title TEXT NOT NULL,
  service_id TEXT,
  total_count INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (total_count > 0),
  used_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (used_count >= 0 AND used_count <= total_count),
  expires_at INTEGER,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active' CHECK (status IN ('active','void')),
  recorded_by TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX idx_benefits_customer ON customer_benefits(customer_id, status);

CREATE TABLE contact_roles (            -- keyed like customer_profiles: 'p:<threadId>'
  zalo_user_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  role TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('customer','manager','staff')),
  source TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (source IN ('zalo_label','staff')),
  updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  updated_by TEXT NOT NULL
);

ALTER TABLE conversations ADD COLUMN auto_reply TEXT CHECK (auto_reply IS NULL OR auto_reply IN ('on','off'));
ALTER TABLE conversations ADD COLUMN room_channel_id TEXT;
ALTER TABLE conversations ADD COLUMN room_synced_at INTEGER;

ALTER TABLE messages ADD COLUMN room_mirrored_at INTEGER;   -- NULL = not yet copied into the room
UPDATE messages SET room_mirrored_at = 0;                   -- history before this migration is never replayed
CREATE INDEX idx_messages_room_pending ON messages(conversation_id, seq) WHERE room_mirrored_at IS NULL;
```

The table copy names the old columns explicitly (the old `visits` has `id, customer_id, service_id, visited_at, source, external_visit_id, booking_request_id, recorded_by, created_at`). Old migration files are never edited. Checked on 2026-11-12 on throwaway databases with the `d1 migrations apply --local --persist-to` command of `scripts/start_local.sh`: 0001 to 0010 apply in order on an empty database; applied to a database stopped at 0009 with rows, 0010 keeps every visit row with its values (new columns NULL), sets `room_mirrored_at = 0` on the old messages (a later message gets NULL) and leaves `PRAGMA foreign_key_check` empty. The build did not touch the real `~/.thomhouse-bot/d1`; `scripts/start_local.sh` applies pending migrations there when it starts the server.

Before 0010 is applied (a running `wrangler dev` reloads code but not the schema), the customer path keeps working and treats a missing table or column as "no role, no switch, no summary, rooms off" (`isMissingMigration0010`, `orMissing0010` in `src/lib/roles.ts`): message ingest, claim, heartbeat and draft acceptance run as before; `getContactRole` answers null, `getContactRoles` an empty map; `ConversationDetail` has no `customer_summary` and no `room`; `get_customer_profile` answers `{ "found": false, "reason": "no_data" }`; `POST /runner/rooms/feed` answers `{ "enabled": false, "conversations": [] }`. These answer 409 `invalid_state` with the message "the database is older than section 14: apply migration 0010_rooms_roles_customer.sql": `POST /runner/rooms/ack`, `PUT /staff/conversations/:id/auto-reply` (both values), every staff endpoint of 14.3 and 14.5 (a dry run of `POST /staff/visits/import` too) and `POST /staff/visits` with one of the new fields. `PATCH /staff/customers/:id` and a `POST /staff/visits` body without the new fields work before 0010.

### 14.3 Contact roles (Zalo labels `Khách hàng`, `Quản lý`, `Nhân viên`)

`contact_roles` holds at most one row per contact: `role` in `customer | manager | staff`, `source` in `zalo_label | staff`. The key is `p:` plus 1 to 32 digits (`ROLE_ID_RE`). A row with `source = 'staff'` (set in the console) is never overwritten or removed by a label import. `src/lib/roles.ts`:

```ts
export type ContactRole = 'customer' | 'manager' | 'staff';
export interface ContactRoleRow { zalo_user_id: string; role: ContactRole; source: 'zalo_label' | 'staff'; updated_at: number; updated_by: string }
/** null when there is no row or the table is missing (migration 0010 not applied). */
export async function getContactRole(env: Env, zaloUserId: string): Promise<ContactRoleRow | null>;
/** Rows of several contacts (conversation lists); an empty map when the table is missing. */
export async function getContactRoles(env: Env, zaloUserIds: string[]): Promise<Map<string, ContactRoleRow>>;
/** manager or staff. */
export function isInternalRole(role: ContactRole | null | undefined): boolean;
/** true when the contact of this conversation has the role manager or staff; false before 0010. */
export async function conversationIsInternal(env: Env, conversationId: string): Promise<boolean>;
```

Effects:

- **Internal contact** (`manager` or `staff`). No automatic path sends anything to this contact and nothing is raised for staff:
  - Ingest stores the inbound message, then stops before the consent gate and before any fixed text. It cancels the queued draft jobs of the conversation, clears the debounce and its alarm, turns the queued automatic bridge rows (bot replies, also ones staff approved before the role was set, and server messages such as a consent prompt or a follow-up) into `failed` with error `cancelled: internal contact` without `needs_attention` (every bridge claim repeats this for all conversations; rows typed or requeued by staff stay queued), and returns `IngestResult.action = 'internal_contact'` (additive). No `needs_attention`, no handoff, no consent prompt; `bot_state` is not changed.
  - `draftBlocker` answers `internal_contact` before every other blocker, at enqueue and at claim, without `needs_attention` or handoff. A leased job gets `cancel: true` with the existing `cancel_reason: 'job_cancelled'` on its next heartbeat (the job row records `last_error = 'cancel:internal_contact'`), so the runner needs no new value.
  - A draft that still arrives is stored as `discarded` with action `dropped`; the re-check right before an auto-send does the same. Approving a bot draft of the contact answers 409 `invalid_state`. Write tools of a running job answer `is_error: true` with `{ "status": "rejected", "error": "internal_contact" }`; read tools still run, and `get_customer_profile` answers `found: false` with reason `internal_contact`.
  - `POST /staff/visits` still records the visit; its `thank_you` and `check_in` jobs are born `skipped` with `last_error = 'internal_contact'`, and the scheduler skips a follow-up queued before the role was set. A failed job, an expired lease and the offline-runner sweep open no handoff; `needs_attention` ignores the contact's unanswered messages.
  - No row in the rooms feed (14.7); `PUT .../auto-reply` with `on` answers 409.
  - An expired staff takeover is not released while the role is set. An outbound message the owner types to the contact on the phone still starts the usual 12 h `staff_takeover` (section 12).
  - Staff can still write to the contact: manual send, staff menu images, and a `requeue` of a cancelled outbox row. A row the bridge has already leased when the role is set is not recalled.
  - Removing the role restores the normal flow on the next message. A text that starts with `/` from a `staff` contact is reserved for staff commands (`/tour`, `/tip`, `/lich-off`); they are not built and the text is stored like any other message.
- **`customer`**: overrides the analyzer's relationship for the bot. The quiet rule of section 13 does not fire, and `profileFactsForModel` and `customerFactsForModel` (14.6) read the profile as relationship `customer` even when the stored relationship is `family`, `staff`, `friend` or `personal`. The consent gate still applies.
- **No row**: section 13 applies unchanged.

Every gate above reads `contact_roles` only. A contact whose section 13 profile says `staff` and who has no row is a normal contact for the gates, the switch and the feed, while `get_customer_profile` answers `internal_contact` for it (open decision, Known gaps).

Staff API (staff auth):

| Method | Path | Body | 200 | Errors |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/staff/roles/import` | `{ "source": "zalo_label", "complete": true, "roles": [{ "zalo_user_id": "p:123", "role": "customer" }] }`, at most 5 000 entries | `{ "imported": n, "updated": n, "unchanged": n, "removed": n, "kept_staff": n }` | 422 (whole list refused; `details.field` names the first bad field): body not an object, `source` other than `zalo_label`, `complete` not a boolean, more than 5 000 entries, bad id, bad role, an id listed twice; 409 before 0010 |
| GET | `/staff/roles` | — | `{ "roles": [ContactRoleRow] }`, last change first | 409 before 0010 |
| PUT | `/staff/roles/:zalo_user_id` | `{ "role": "customer" \| "manager" \| "staff" \| null }` | `{ "role": ContactRoleRow \| null }` (`source = 'staff'`; `null` deletes the row whatever its source, so the next label import applies again) | 400 bad id, 422 bad or missing role, 409 before 0010 |

`complete: true` deletes the `zalo_label` rows that are not in the list (the label was removed in Zalo); `kept_staff` counts list entries skipped because a staff row exists; `updated` counts label rows whose role changed. The import writes in D1 batches of at most 500 statements, and its upsert never overwrites a staff row written between its read and its write. Audit `roles.import` (counts and `complete` only) and `role.staff_edit` (role and source values only; `before` is null for a first edit). `CustomerProfileView` and `ConversationSummary` (list and detail) gain optional `role` and `role_source`, emitted only when a `contact_roles` row exists, so `role_source` there is `zalo_label` or `staff`, never `profile`.

Label sync (bridge, `bridge/labels.py`, command `cd apps/zalo-bridge && python3 -m bridge.main sync-labels [--dry-run]`): runs `zalo-agent --json label list` through `run_engine` (45 s timeout). The output (shape checked against the real engine on 2026-11-12, values not printed) is `{ "labelData": [{ "id": int, "text": string, "conversations": [string], "color", "emoji", "offset", "createTime" }], "version": int, "lastUpdateTime": int }`. A personal conversation id of 1 to 32 digits becomes `p:<id>`; a group id (starts with `g`) is counted in `skipped.groups`, any other id (also a digit string longer than 32) in `skipped.other_ids`, and neither is posted. The whole label text is normalised (NFD without marks, `đ` to `d`, lower case, single spaces) and must equal `khach hang` (`customer`), `quan ly` (`manager`) or `nhan vien` (`staff`); other labels are ignored. A contact under several of the three labels gets the first of manager, staff, customer. The result is posted to `/staff/roles/import` with `complete: true`. Nothing is posted when the engine fails or times out, the document has no `labelData`, a role label's `conversations` is not a list of strings, or the list has more than 5 000 roles; a well-formed empty list is complete and is posted. Auth: `STAFF_TOKEN` from the environment, else from `apps/server/.dev.vars` (`THOMHOUSE_DEV_VARS` overrides the path) only for a local `SERVER_URL`; `X-Staff-Id: label-sync`. Exit codes: 0 synced (or a dry run read the labels), 1 engine or server failure, 2 no staff token. `python3 -m bridge.main run` runs the sync at start and every `LABEL_SYNC_EVERY_S` seconds (bridge `.env`, default 900, 0 disables it) on a daemon thread; a failing sync logs one line and never stops message delivery. Output and logs carry counts only, never label texts, names or ids. Not verified live: whether `label list` runs while the listener holds the account's session.

### 14.4 Auto reply switch per conversation

`conversations.auto_reply` is `'on'`, `'off'` or NULL (never set). `conversationMode(flags, conv)` in `src/lib/flags.ts` replaces every direct `effectiveMode(flags.auto_reply_mode, conv.mode)` call (draft blocker, claim turn context, heartbeat, auto-send decision and its re-check, approve, staff views); `effectiveMode` is now called only inside `flags.ts`. A row read before 0010 has no `auto_reply` and counts as NULL.

1. Global flag `auto_reply_mode = 'off'`: `off` (the global stop always wins).
2. `auto_reply = 'on'`: `auto_allowed`, whatever the global flag (other than `off`) and `conv.mode` say.
3. `auto_reply = 'off'`: the lower of `effectiveMode(global, conv.mode)` and `approve_each`.
4. NULL: `effectiveMode(global, conv.mode)`, unchanged.

So with the default global `approve_each`: switch off means the bots draft and staff approve, edit or answer themselves; a conversation or global mode of `off` means the bots only observe (14.7); switch on means the reply is sent without approval when every auto-send condition holds: the draft is not superseded, the checker passed, the mode is `auto_allowed`, `bot_state` is `bot_active`, the job made no `handoff_to_staff` or `ai_assistant` opt-out call, no menu image is attached, `outbound_enabled` is true, and the customer's last interaction is at most 7 days old. Right before delivery the DO re-reads the conversation, the flags and the role and drops the auto-send when one of them changed. A draft that misses a condition waits as `pending` (`blocked` when the checker failed) for approval, as before. On the personal channel the bridge pacing and the 30-minute queue expiry of section 12 still apply to an auto-sent reply.

`PUT /staff/conversations/:id/auto-reply`, body `{ "auto_reply": "on" | "off" }`, answers `{ "conversation": ConversationSummary }`. `on` also sets `mode = NULL`; `off` keeps `mode`. 404 unknown conversation; 422 another value; 409 `invalid_state` for `on` on an internal contact, and for both values before 0010. Audit `conversation.auto_reply` (`before`/`after`: `auto_reply` and `mode`). `PUT /staff/conversations/:id/mode` with `off`, `draft_only` or `approve_each` on a conversation whose switch is on sets `auto_reply = 'off'` in the same batch and writes a second audit row `conversation.auto_reply` with `after: { "auto_reply": "off", "by": "mode" }`; `null` and `auto_allowed` keep the switch on. `ConversationSummary` gains optional `auto_reply: 'on' | 'off'` (only when set).

### 14.5 Visits, benefits, phone

`POST /staff/visits` gains optional `amount_vnd` (integer, 0 or more), `technician` (one line, at most 60 characters), `duration_min` (integer, 1 to 1 440), `service_name` (one line, at most 120 characters) and `benefit_id`. Absent, null and an empty text mean "not given"; a bad value answers 422 naming the field. `service_id` must still be a service of the active catalog (422 otherwise). The benefit must belong to the same customer, be `active`, have a use left and not have expired on the local date of `visited_at` (see "Benefits" below), else 409 `invalid_state`; the visit row and `used_count + 1` are written in one batch, and when a concurrent request took the last use the `CHECK` of `customer_benefits` rolls the batch back with 409 (implemented, not covered by a test). It still creates the `thank_you` and `check_in` jobs (born `skipped` for an opted-out customer or an internal contact). A body without the new fields runs the 9-column `INSERT` of before section 14. Audit `visit.record` with `service_id` and `benefit_id`.

A visit recorded long after it happened is not followed up late. Every `thank_you` and `check_in` job that `recordVisit` creates carries `max_lateness_ms = FOLLOWUP_MAX_LATENESS_MS` (6 hours, `src/jobs/followup-jobs.ts`). `runFollowupJob` (`src/scheduler/followups.ts`, rule 2 of its decision order) marks a job `expired` with `last_error = 'too_late'` when `now > run_at + max_lateness_ms`, after the kill-switch check and before the send window, consent, template and send steps. While `scheduled_sends_enabled` or `outbound_enabled` is false, the scheduler returns before that check and the job stays `queued` with its `run_at` in the past; the first run after both flags allow a send expires it. A visit recorded within the bound can still be followed up up to 6 hours after its `run_at`. The live run-through of 2026-11-12 created such jobs for a visit 23 days old (nothing was sent: no `followup_care` consent, flag off); `test/followup-lateness.test.ts` pins the rule (a 23-day-old visit: both jobs `expired`/`too_late`, no outbox row; a visit of yesterday: the thank-you is sent).

`POST /staff/visits/import` records history and never creates follow-up jobs:

```json
{ "dry_run": false, "visits": [{
  "customer_id": "cus_…", "zalo_user_id": "p:123", "phone": "0901234567",
  "service_id": "massage_chan_60", "service_name": "Massage chân 60 phút",
  "visited_at": 1760000000000, "visited_at_local": "2026-10-09 14:30",
  "amount_vnd": 450000, "technician": "Hoa", "duration_min": 60, "external_visit_id": "pos-991" }] }
```

- Shape first, 422 for the whole request naming the field: the body is an object, `dry_run` a boolean, `visits` an array of at most 2 000 objects, and in each row `external_visit_id` a text of at most 100 characters, `technician` one line of at most 60 characters, `duration_min` an integer from 1 to 1 440 (`details.field` like `visits[3].technician`).
- Then each row gets the first reason that applies, in this order, in `unmatched`:
  - `no_customer`: the first of `customer_id`, `zalo_user_id`, `phone` that is given decides; there is no fall-through to the next key. `customer_id`: an existing customer that is not deleted. `zalo_user_id`: exactly one customer row with that id, not deleted; a `p:` + 1-32 digits id without a row is accepted and gets a minimal `customers` row (`id`, `oa_id = 'personal'`, `zalo_user_id`, `channel = 'personal'`, `created_at`; no name, no phone) only when at least one of its rows is imported, counted in `created_customers`. `phone`: exactly one customer, not deleted, whose stored phone normalises to the same digits.
  - `bad_time`: `visited_at` (ms; when given, `visited_at_local` is ignored) or `visited_at_local` (`YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM` in Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh; a `T` instead of the space, seconds and a one-digit hour are accepted; a date alone is not) is missing, not a real time, before 2000-01-01 or more than one hour after now.
  - `bad_service`: `service_id` is not in the active catalog and `service_name` is missing, or `service_name` is given but is not a one-line text of at most 120 characters, or has no letter or digit. `service_name` is never matched against catalog names: a row without a catalog `service_id` is stored with `service_id = 'ext:' + slug(service_name)` (slug: lower-case ASCII letters and digits joined by `-`, at most 60 characters) and `service_name`.
  - `bad_amount`: `amount_vnd` is given and is not an integer of 0 or more.
- `source = 'import'`. A row whose `external_visit_id` exists (in the table or earlier in the request), or, without one, whose customer, `visited_at` and `service_id` match an existing visit or an earlier row of the request, is counted in `duplicates` and not written.
- Response `{ "imported": n, "duplicates": n, "created_customers": n, "unmatched": [{ "index": 0, "reason": "no_customer" | "bad_time" | "bad_service" | "bad_amount" }] }`. `dry_run: true` answers the same counts and writes nothing.
- Writes go in D1 batches of at most 500 statements; the import is not one transaction, so a failure in a later batch leaves the earlier batches written, and a re-run counts those rows as duplicates. The insert is a plain `INSERT`: a concurrent write of the same `external_visit_id` fails that batch. The request loads `customers` (id, Zalo id, phone) once to match rows. Audit `visits.import` with counts only, also for an empty list.

`GET /staff/visits?customer_id=` answers `{ "visits": [VisitView] }`, newest first, at most 200 rows; `VisitView` is the row (`id, customer_id, service_id, service_name, visited_at, duration_min, amount_vnd, technician, benefit_id, source, external_visit_id, recorded_by, created_at`) plus `name` (the active catalog's `display_name_vi`, else `service_name`, else `service_id`). 422 without `customer_id`, 404 for an unknown or deleted customer.

Benefits (ids `ben_`, `newId('ben')`):

- `GET /staff/benefits?customer_id=` answers `{ "benefits": [CustomerBenefit] }`, active ones first, then newest first.
- `POST /staff/benefits` with `{ customer_id, kind: 'package' | 'voucher', title, service_id?, total_count, used_count?, expires_at? }` answers `{ "benefit": CustomerBenefit }`. `title` is one line of 1 to 120 characters, `total_count` an integer from 1 to 1 000, `used_count` from 0 to `total_count` (default 0), `expires_at` ms or null (a past time is accepted, for an expired package recorded late), `service_id` a service of the active catalog.
- `PATCH /staff/benefits/:id` with one or more of `title`, `used_count` (0 to `total_count`), `expires_at` (ms or null), `status` (`active` | `void`) answers `{ "benefit": CustomerBenefit }`; 422 for an unknown field, an empty body or a bad value, 404 for an unknown benefit.
- `CustomerBenefit` is the row plus `remaining = total_count - used_count` and `expires_on` (local date of `expires_at`). A benefit is usable while it is `active`, has a use left and `expires_at` is null or not before local midnight of the day in question, so it can be used through the whole local expiry date.
- GET and POST answer 422 without a customer and 404 for an unknown or deleted customer. Audit `benefit.create` (without the title) and `benefit.update` (the changed values; a title change only as `title_changed`).

Phone: `PATCH /staff/customers/:id` with `{ "phone": "0901234567" | null }` answers `{ "customer": { id, channel, zalo_user_id, display_name, phone, minor_flag, last_user_interaction_at } }`. The number is stored as digits (`normalizePhone`): spaces, dots, dashes and parentheses are removed, a leading `+84` becomes `0`, so does a leading `84` of an 11-digit number, and 9 to 11 digits must remain; `null` clears it. 422 for a bad number, a body without `phone` or with another field; 404 for an unknown or deleted customer. Audit `customer.phone_edit` says only whether a number is stored. `create_booking_request` copies a phone the customer typed (`contact_method: 'phone_shared_with_consent'`, the existing "phone came from the customer" check) into `customers.phone` only when `normalizePhone` accepts it and the stored phone is NULL or blank; the booking request keeps the text as typed.

### 14.6 Customer status and summary

`src/lib/customer-status.ts` is pure (no `Env`, `now` is an argument):

```ts
export interface VisitFact { visited_at: number; service_id: string; amount_vnd: number | null }
export interface StatusConfig { absentFactor: number; minAbsentDays: number; defaultGapDays: number; windowDays: number }
export const DEFAULT_STATUS_CONFIG: StatusConfig = { absentFactor: 2, minAbsentDays: 14, defaultGapDays: 45, windowDays: 183 };
export type CustomerStatus = 'first_inquiry' | 'first_visit_done' | 'returning' | 'long_absent';
export interface ServiceCount { service_id: string; count: number }
export interface CustomerStatusResult {
  status: CustomerStatus;
  visits_total: number;                 // visit days
  last_visit_at: number | null;
  days_since_last_visit: number | null;
  usual_gap_days: number | null;
  gap_basis: 'own' | 'service_peers' | 'default' | null;
  absent_after_days: number | null;
  window: { days: number; from: number; visits: number; spend_vnd: number; spend_visits: number; avg_spend_vnd: number | null; services: ServiceCount[] };
  lifetime: { spend_vnd: number; spend_visits: number; services: ServiceCount[] };
}
export function computeCustomerStatus(visits: VisitFact[], now: number,
  opts?: { config?: Partial<StatusConfig>; peerGapDays?: number | null }): CustomerStatusResult;
```

Rules, all on local dates (Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh):

1. A visit day is a local date with at least one visit row; several services on one day are one visit. Rows with the same `visited_at` and `service_id` are one row (the known amount is kept, the larger one when both are known). `visits_total` counts visit days.
2. No visit day: `first_inquiry` (the contact wrote but no visit is recorded); every gap field is null.
3. A gap is the number of days between two consecutive visit days. `usual_gap_days` is the exact median of the customer's own gaps (`gap_basis = 'own'`): the middle gap, or the mean of the two middle gaps when their number is even, so the value can end in `.5`. With a single visit day it is `peerGapDays` when given (`service_peers`), else `defaultGapDays` (`default`).
4. `absent_after_days = max(minAbsentDays, round(absentFactor × usual_gap_days))`. The threshold follows each customer's own rhythm; no fixed number of days marks a customer as lost.
5. `days_since_last_visit` is the number of local dates from the last visit day to today. `days_since_last_visit > absent_after_days`: `long_absent`. Otherwise one visit day is `first_visit_done` and two or more are `returning`.
6. Window: the visit days from local midnight of the local date of `now − windowDays` through `now` (183 days cover 184 local dates); `window.from` is that midnight. `spend_vnd` sums the known amounts (null is unknown, never 0). `spend_visits` counts the visit days with at least one known amount; such a day adds the sum of its known amounts. `avg_spend_vnd = round(spend_vnd / spend_visits)` or null. `ServiceCount.count` is the number of visit days on which the service appears; `services` is sorted by count, then id. `lifetime` is the same over all visits. Spend levels are compared inside the window, never by lifetime total, so a new customer is not ranked against a long-standing one.

`peerGapDays` (`src/lib/customer-summary.ts`) is the median, over the other customers with two or more visit days who used one of the services of this customer's visits (at most 80 service ids), of their own median gaps; null with fewer than three such customers. Config from optional env integers, read by `statusConfigFromEnv`: `CUSTOMER_ABSENT_FACTOR_PCT` (200, so the factor is 2), `CUSTOMER_MIN_ABSENT_DAYS` (14), `CUSTOMER_DEFAULT_GAP_DAYS` (45), `CUSTOMER_WINDOW_DAYS` (183); a missing, non-numeric or non-positive value gives the default. They are not listed in `wrangler.toml` (optional integers with code defaults stay out of `[vars]`, like `PERSONAL_SEND_MAX_PER_HOUR`); locally they go into `apps/server/.dev.vars`.

```ts
/** Staff view. null when the customer does not exist or is deleted; null before migration 0010. */
export async function buildCustomerSummary(env: Env, customerId: string, now: number): Promise<CustomerSummary | null>;
/** Model view of the same data for the tool get_customer_profile. */
export async function customerFactsForModel(env: Env, customerId: string, catalogVersion: string, now: number): Promise<CustomerFacts>;
```

`CustomerSummary` (optional `customer_summary` on `ConversationDetail`, left out when null):

```json
{ "role": "customer", "role_source": "zalo_label",
  "name": "Lan", "address_term": "chị", "phone": "0901234567",
  "status": "returning", "visits_total": 7,
  "last_visit": { "visited_at": 1761800000000, "date": "2026-10-30", "time": "14:30",
                  "services": [{ "service_id": "massage_chan_60", "name": "Massage chân 60 phút" }],
                  "technician": "Hoa", "amount_vnd": 450000 },
  "days_since_last_visit": 13, "usual_gap_days": 21, "gap_basis": "own", "absent_after_days": 42,
  "lifetime_spend_vnd": 2950000, "lifetime_spend_visits": 6,
  "window": { "days": 183, "from_date": "2026-05-13", "visits": 3, "spend_vnd": 1350000, "avg_spend_vnd": 450000,
              "services": [{ "service_id": "massage_chan_60", "name": "Massage chân 60 phút", "count": 3 }] },
  "benefits": [{ "id": "ben_…", "kind": "package", "title": "Gói 10 buổi gội dưỡng sinh", "remaining": 4, "expires_on": "2027-01-31" }] }
```

- `role`/`role_source`: the `contact_roles` row (`zalo_label` or `staff`) when there is one; otherwise derived from the section 13 relationship at any confidence (`customer` gives `customer`, `staff` gives `staff`, `role_source = 'profile'`); otherwise null.
- `name`: the profile's `name_to_use`, else `customers.display_name`, else the profile's `display_name`.
- `last_visit` describes the last visit day: `visited_at` and `time` are those of the newest row of that day, `services` the day's services in time order, `technician` the day's technicians joined with `, `, `amount_vnd` the sum of the day's known amounts (null when none is known).
- Service names: the active catalog's `display_name_vi`, else the stored `service_name`, else the service id.
- `benefits` lists the active benefits with a use left that are usable today, soonest expiry first.
- Additive 2026-11-12 (section 15.12): for a contact with chat facts, the visits guessed from old chats at confidence `high` or `medium` count as visit days in every figure above, the summary gains `chat` and `last_visit.from_chat`, and `get_customer_profile` gains `visits_from_chat`, `last_visit.from_chat`, `next_booking_from_chat` and `benefits_from_chat`. For every other contact the summary and the tool answer are unchanged.

`CustomerFacts`, the result of the read tool **`get_customer_profile`** (input `{}`, `additionalProperties: false`; the ninth entry of `TOOL_NAMES`, in `design/tools-schema.json` with an `x_server_policy` entry and in `apps/runner/runner/tools-schema.json`; not a write tool; stored in `tool_calls`). `GET /runner/prompt` lists it, so `prompt_hash` changed on 2026-11-12. The direct `claude -p` engine does not advertise it (`mcp_proxy.TOOL_NAMES` and `--allowedTools mcp__thom` keep the eight tools); only the rooms tools server (14.8) does.

```json
{ "found": true, "role": "customer", "address_term": "chị", "name_to_use": "Lan", "has_phone": true,
  "status": "returning", "visits_total": 7,
  "last_visit": { "date": "2026-10-30", "time": "14:30", "services": ["Massage chân 60 phút"], "other_services": 0, "technician": "Hoa" },
  "days_since_last_visit": 13, "usual_gap_days": 21, "gap_basis": "own", "absent_after_days": 42,
  "window": { "days": 183, "visits": 3, "spend_vnd": 1350000, "avg_spend_vnd": 450000,
              "usual_services": [{ "name": "Massage chân 60 phút", "count": 3 }], "other_services": 0 },
  "lifetime": { "spend_vnd": 2950000, "spend_visits": 6 },
  "unused_benefits": [{ "kind": "package", "title": "Gói 10 buổi gội dưỡng sinh", "remaining": 4, "expires_on": "2027-01-31" }],
  "services_mentioned": ["Gội đầu dưỡng sinh"] }
```

Differences from the staff view:

- No phone, only `has_phone`.
- A service is named only when section 13 rule 4 allows it: a service of the catalog `catalogVersion` (the active one when the argument is empty) outside the `health`/`women` groups. Every other service, `ext:` ids included, is only counted in `other_services`; `usual_services` holds at most 5 names (`MAX_MODEL_SERVICES`), and services beyond that are counted too.
- `address_term`, `name_to_use` and `services_mentioned` come from `modelFactsFromRow` (section 13 rules 2 to 4), with a `customer` role read as relationship `customer`.
- The last visit has no age cap (the 180-day cap of rule 5 belongs to the greeting template of the direct engine).
- A benefit title is free text typed by staff. It is replaced by `Gói dịch vụ` (package) or `Voucher` when the benefit's service is in a `health`/`women` group or not in the catalog, or when the title contains the name of a catalog service of those groups (compared without diacritics).
- `{ "found": false, "reason": "no_data" }` for a missing or deleted customer, before migration 0010, and for a contact without a `contact_roles` row whose profile relationship is `family`, `friend` or the legacy `personal`. `{ "found": false, "reason": "internal_contact" }` for a `manager`/`staff` row, and for a profile relationship `staff` without a row. A customer without visits and without a profile answers `found: true` with `status: 'first_inquiry'`.

The amounts are for the bots' judgement; the customer bot is told to report a spend level in words (14.8), and the checker already blocks a reply that states an amount other than a tool `price_text`.

### 14.7 Rooms feed (runner API)

Flag `rooms_enabled` (boolean, default `false`, section 1). Both endpoints use the runner bearer token and `X-Runner-Id`.

#### `POST /runner/rooms/feed`

Body `{ "conversation_id"?: string, "seed"?: boolean, "limit"?: number }`; an absent body counts as `{}`. Response:

```json
{ "enabled": true, "conversations": [{
  "conversation_id": "cnv_…", "customer_display_name": "Lan",
  "messages": [{ "message_id": "msg_…", "seq": 41, "source": "customer", "direction": "inbound", "kind": "text", "text": "…", "at": 1762900000000 }] }] }
```

- Flag `false` (the body is not read) or migration 0010 missing: `{ "enabled": false, "conversations": [] }`. 422 for `conversation_id` that is not a text of 1 to 100 characters, `seed` that is not a boolean or is `true` without `conversation_id`, and `limit` that is not an integer of 1 or more (a larger `limit` is cut to 20).
- A conversation is eligible when its customer is on the personal channel and not deleted, `minor_flag` is not 1, the customer's latest `ai_assistant` consent row is `granted`, and no `contact_roles` row gives the contact `manager` or `staff`. Group threads never reach the server (section 12), so every eligible conversation is one-to-one.
- A row meets the row rule when `seq > consent_seq`, `text IS NOT NULL`, `kind != 'user_info'`, it is inbound or has `send_state = 'sent'` (the delivered value), and it is at most 14 days old. It is pending when it also has `room_mirrored_at IS NULL`. `source` is `customer`, `bot`, `staff` or `server`. `customer_display_name` is one line of at most 40 characters without `<`, `>` and `"` (the `oneLine` of the claim's turn context), or null; the feed has no phone field.
- Without `conversation_id` (observation): eligible conversations with pending rows that no draft turn will deliver. A conversation whose pending rows include a customer message and whose `draftBlocker` is null is skipped (its next draft turn delivers them); every other one is listed (mode `off`, staff takeover, `staff_only`, handoff, AI calls off, or no pending customer row). At most `limit` conversations (default 5, at most 20), oldest pending row first, at most 50 rows each. One call scans at most 200 candidate conversations, in pages of 40.
- With `conversation_id` (draft turn): an eligible conversation answers exactly one entry with its pending rows (at most 100, oldest first) whatever the blocker, and `messages: []` when nothing waits; an ineligible or unknown conversation answers `conversations: []`. The runner relies on this difference (14.8 step 0).
- `seed: true` with `conversation_id`: the last 20 rows that meet the row rule, ignoring `room_mirrored_at`, oldest first (context for a new room).

#### `POST /runner/rooms/ack`

Body `{ "conversation_id": "cnv_…", "message_ids": ["msg_…"], "room": { "channel_id": "…" } }` (`room` optional). Sets `room_mirrored_at = now` on the listed rows of that conversation that are still NULL, and `room_channel_id` / `room_synced_at = now` when `room` is given. Response `{ "ok": true, "marked": n }`, where `marked` counts only rows that were still NULL, so a repeated call marks nothing more. 422 for a body that is not an object, `message_ids` that is not a list of non-empty id strings or has more than 200 ids, and a `room.channel_id` that is empty or longer than 200 characters; 404 for an unknown conversation; 409 before 0010. Ack is accepted while `rooms_enabled` is false. `ConversationDetail` gains optional `room: { "channel_id", "synced_at", "pending": n }` when `room_channel_id` is set; `pending` counts the pending rows of an eligible conversation (0 otherwise).

### 14.8 Runner room engine

Runner settings (environment or `apps/runner/.env`; `bridge.main init-env` adds them when missing):

| Variable | Default | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `THOMHOUSE_DRAFT_ENGINE` | `claude` | `claude` = one `claude -p` per draft (sections 3 and 13). `rooms` = the two-bot room described here. Another value stops the runner at start: `config error: THOMHOUSE_DRAFT_ENGINE must be one of claude, rooms` (exit 2) |
| `OPENMAUSBOT_BIN` | `<repo>/tools/openmausbot/node_modules/.bin/openmausbot` | platform CLI |
| `OPENMAUSBOT_DATA_DIR` | `<THOMHOUSE_BOT_HOME>/openmausbot` | platform data folder, passed to the CLI as `OMB_DATA_DIR` |
| `OPENMAUSBOT_NODE_DIR` | empty | directory with a `node` 24 or newer, put first on `PATH` for the CLI |
| `ROOM_TURN_TIMEOUT_S` | `240` | whole two-bot turn; must be greater than 0 |
| `ROOM_OBSERVE_EVERY_S` | `30` | observation loop; `0` disables it |
| `ROOM_FALLBACK` | `claude` | on a room failure: `claude` = draft with the direct engine, `fail` = fail the job |
| `ROOM_REQUIRE_PROFILE_READ` | `1` | reject a reply written without a successful `get_customer_profile` call for the turn (`1`/`0`) |
| `ROOM_CUSTOMER_MODEL` | empty | model of the customer bot; empty = same as the lead |

`Runner` builds `RoomEngine(config, backend, claude_runner)` only when the engine is `rooms`. `RoomEngine` implements `run_job(job, handle) -> Outcome` and forwards `pool_snapshot`, `pool_has_account` and `use_account` to the wrapped `ClaudeRunner`, so account status and the pool keep working; the bots run on the first account the pool would use next. Every platform call goes through one wrapper (`PlatformCli`): `--json`, `OMB_DATA_DIR`, `OPENMAUSBOT_NODE_DIR` first on `PATH`, an environment limited to `HOME`, `USER`, `LOGNAME`, `SHELL`, `LANG`, `TMPDIR` (no runner token, no Claude token), its own process group, a timeout, defensive JSON parsing. Log lines carry ids, exit codes and reason codes, never message text or a ticket.

Files under `<home>/rooms/` (folder and `tickets/` mode 0700, files 0600): `team.json` (generated team file; JSON is valid YAML), `state.json` (`prompt_hash`, `instance`, `model`, `customer_model`, `fingerprint`, `applied_at`), `tools.json` (the server's tool definitions from `GET /runner/prompt`), `<cnv_id>.json` (`channel_id`, `room`, `seeded`, and since the evening of 2026-11-12 `bot_replied`, see "First reply" below; removed by `python3 -m runner.main forget <cnv_id>`), `credentials.json` (API URL, runner id, token; read only by the tools server), `tickets/<ticket>.json` (`job_id`, `conversation_id`, `expires_at` = now + `ROOM_TURN_TIMEOUT_S` + 60 s), `tickets/<ticket>.calls.jsonl`, and the working folders `work/lead` and `work/customer`. Ticket files older than one hour are removed when a new ticket is written.

A draft turn (one per leased job, under a per-conversation lock shared with the observation loop):

0. Read the feed for the conversation once. `enabled: false`: the direct engine runs the job and no platform call is made. No entry for the conversation (not eligible, 14.7): the direct engine runs the job with no platform call, no ticket and no ack, whatever `ROOM_FALLBACK` says.
1. Build the team document and compare its fingerprint (defaults, teams, bots, lead `CLAUDE.md` text) with `state.json`. When it differs, or the conversation has no room map file, write `team.json` with the two bots and this one room (`zalo-<cnv_id>`, responder `mentions`) and run `team apply <team.json> --wait-idle 30s --json`, never with `prune`. Find the room's channel with `channels --json` by name and store it in the map file. After every apply, and before any other draft turn whose cheap check fails, place the lead's reply rules in the lead bot's platform workspace ("Lead rules file" below).
2. A new room (`seeded` false) first gets the `seed` feed rows that are not part of this turn, as one unmentioned message headed "Bối cảnh: …" (no bot answers it); then those rows are acked.
3. Create the ticket `tk_<32 hex>` bound to the leased job and write its file.
4. Post one message with `send --channel <id> - --send-id <send id> --json` (text on stdin): the mention line `@Bot dịch vụ`, the line `[phiếu: tk_…]`, `turn_context_text`, the customer profile block of section 13 (`profile_block`), the pending rows that are not in `new_customer_messages` under "Từ lượt trước đến giờ …" (`[Khách 14:01] …`, `[Lễ tân 14:02] …`, `[Bot 14:03] …`, `[Hệ thống 14:03] …`; `dd/mm HH:MM` for an earlier day), the unsent drafts of `unsent_drafts_since_last` under "Các bản nháp trước của bạn CHƯA được gửi …", one line saying whether a bot message has already reached the customer (`FIRST_REPLY_NOTE`, or `LATER_REPLY_NOTE`, which tells the lead not to introduce itself again; added the evening of 2026-11-12, see "First reply" below), then "Tin khách cần trả lời:" with each new customer message in `<customer_message>…</customer_message>` (plus an attachment note). Copied text goes through the `neutralize` helper of the direct engine; `@` becomes `＠` and an opening `[phiếu` becomes `［phiếu`, also in the turn context and the profile block, so that customer text can never wake a bot or imitate the ticket line. The send id is `thom-turn-<job_id>-a<attempt>` (hashed to 40 hex characters when it would not be 16 to 80 characters of letters, digits, `-` and `_`), so a retry inside one attempt never posts twice while a new attempt can post a new turn. Then ack the pending feed rows and the ids of `new_customer_messages`, with the room's channel id.
5. Wait with `wait --channel <id> --for job --since <at> --timeout <left>s --json`, at most four times, with heartbeats; stop at `ROOM_TURN_TIMEOUT_S`. Exit 4 or the runner's own timeout is a timeout; another non-zero exit, unreadable output, or four answers without a completed job and without a bot text are a room failure. After any cancel, lost lease, timeout, failed or cut-short send, or failed wait, once the turn message may have been posted, `interrupt --channel <id> --tree` stops the room job. A failed send also deletes the conversation's room map file and `state.json`, so the next turn applies the team and looks the room up again.
6. Validate: the reply is the last bot text message after the send (`role` bot, `kind` text), or, without messages, the lead root node's result; it must come from `Bot dịch vụ`, not be empty, contain no ticket (`tk_` + hex, `[phiếu`, `［phiếu`), no bot mention (`@Bot`, `＠Bot`), neither bot name even without an at-sign, and not the marker `KHONG-CO-QUY-TAC`; and (with `ROOM_REQUIRE_PROFILE_READ`) the ticket's call log must hold a successful `get_customer_profile`. Then `POST /runner/jobs/:id/draft` with `session_id = "room." + <cnv_id>`, the account label, model, effort, zero usage counts and `cost_usd_equiv` 0 (the platform gives none), `duration_ms`, and `num_turns` = the number of bot executions (at least 1).
7. Always delete the ticket file and its call log. On any failure before a valid reply: `ROOM_FALLBACK = claude` runs the wrapped engine on a copy of the job whose `conversation.session_id` is cleared when it starts with `room.` and whose `history` comes from the `seed` feed (without the new customer messages); `fail` posts `/fail` with `error_kind` `internal` or `timeout`. A cancelled job or a lost lease posts nothing.

The direct engine never resumes a session id that starts with `room.` (`sessions.plan`): after `THOMHOUSE_DRAFT_ENGINE` goes back to `claude`, it starts a new session (reason `room_session`) and the draft carries `error_kind: "session_restarted"`.

Observation loop (its own daemon thread, every `ROOM_OBSERVE_EVERY_S`, only with the `rooms` engine): `feed` without a conversation, then for each conversation whose lock is free make sure the room exists (seeding a new one), post the rows as one unmentioned message headed "Bối cảnh: tin mới … Không bot nào cần trả lời tin này." (no bot runs; the bots read it on their next turn) and ack. While the server answers `enabled: false` a tick does nothing and the loop asks again after max(`ROOM_OBSERVE_EVERY_S`, 300 s), so turning the flag on needs no runner restart. The loop never raises into the main loop.

Tools server `runner/room_tools.py`: a stdio MCP server (stdlib only, single file), started by the platform for each bot turn as `<interpreter that ran rooms-setup> -I <absolute path>/room_tools.py` with `THOMHOUSE_BOT_HOME` in its configured environment; server name `thom`, so tools are `mcp__thom__<name>` as in the direct engine. It advertises the nine tools (definitions from `<home>/rooms/tools.json` when present, else `runner/tools-schema.json`), each with a required string `ticket` added to its schema. On a call it checks the ticket (`tk_` + 32 hex, whole match), reads the ticket file, checks `expires_at`, removes `ticket`, forwards the rest to `POST /runner/tools/{name}` with the ticket's `job_id` and the credentials file (20 s timeout), and appends `{ tool, is_error, bot, at }` to the ticket's call log (`bot` is the name of the working folder, `lead` or `customer`). An unknown, expired or malformed ticket answers `{ "error": "ticket_invalid" }` as a tool error and contacts nothing. A missing credentials file answers a tool error, is logged with `is_error: true` and forwards nothing. The model never sees the token; the bots have no file or shell tools.
